    "dryRun": false,

    "selection": "all",
    "artifacts": [
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "bind-runtime",
        "title": "Bind Runtime",
        "relativePath": "skills/bind-runtime/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"bind-runtime\"\ndescription: \"Map runtime hook capabilities to canonical gate bindings.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=bind-runtime source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Bind Runtime\n\nMap runtime hook capabilities to canonical gate bindings.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: runtime, binding, adapter, hook\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: runtime capability inspection, gate binding status, blocking capability report\n- Out of scope: adapter file generation, platform process management, policy override\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, known_gap\n- Hooks: runtime-bindings, platform-adapters\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "build-inner-loop",
        "title": "Build Inner Loop",
        "relativePath": "skills/build-inner-loop/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"build-inner-loop\"\ndescription: \"Run a risk-calibrated RED/GREEN/REFACTOR implementation loop.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=build-inner-loop source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Build Inner Loop\n\nRun a risk-calibrated RED/GREEN/REFACTOR implementation loop.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: build\n- Gate types: pre_tool, post_tool\n- Risk classes: L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: build, tdd, implement, code\n- Automatic: false\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: test-first workflow, minimum implementation, focused verification\n- Out of scope: release decision, production monitoring, human approval\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: integration_tests, command_output, files_modified\n- Hooks: convergence, evidence-management\n- Subagents: test-writer, reviewer\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "classify-risk",
        "title": "Classify Risk",
        "relativePath": "skills/classify-risk/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"classify-risk\"\ndescription: \"Assign a canonical T/L/M/H/C risk class and operating mode to each intent.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=classify-risk source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Classify Risk\n\nAssign a canonical T/L/M/H/C risk class and operating mode to each intent.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: classify, risk, intent\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: risk classification, forcing signal scan, operating mode selection\n- Out of scope: cycle transition, implementation, evidence sufficiency evaluation\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: risk-classification\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "close-run",
        "title": "Close Run",
        "relativePath": "skills/close-run/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"close-run\"\ndescription: \"Finalize a run only after evidence, policy, and runtime gates agree.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=close-run source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Close Run\n\nFinalize a run only after evidence, policy, and runtime gates agree.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: stop\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: close, final, finish\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: final state selection, residual risk summary, known gap disclosure\n- Out of scope: additional implementation, silent evidence fabrication, risk downgrade\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: confidence_level, known_gap, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: close-finalization, evidence-management, gate-policy\n- Subagents: evidence-collector\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "gate-policy",
        "title": "Gate Policy",
        "relativePath": "skills/gate-policy/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"gate-policy\"\ndescription: \"Evaluate lifecycle gates against risk policy, runtime bindings, and allowed scope.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=gate-policy source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Gate Policy\n\nEvaluate lifecycle gates against risk policy, runtime bindings, and allowed scope.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt, pre_tool, post_tool, pre_compact, post_compact, stop, subagent_start, subagent_stop\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: gate, policy, guard\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: gate decisions, policy violations, context injection\n- Out of scope: risk scoring, subagent implementation, adapter-specific hook install\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, known_gap\n- Hooks: gate-policy, runtime-bindings, platform-adapters\n- Subagents: evidence-collector\n"

      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "hima-enter",
        "title": "HIMA Enter",
        "relativePath": "skills/hima-enter/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"hima-enter\"\ndescription: \"Start a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=hima-enter source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# HIMA Enter\n\nStart a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: hima, enter, start development, development mode, mode developpement, governed development\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Procedure\n\nUse this skill as the first explicit step before governed implementation work. Do not depend on OMX, external workflow skills, or manual edits to `.planning/`.\n\n1. Decide whether the user is asking for development. If the request is only chat, research, explanation, or status, do not enter `build/Execute`; report status instead.\n2. Choose one operating mode: `bypass` for T/L low-friction work only, `auto` as the default autonomous development mode, or `pairing` when the user wants checkpoints or the risk is H/C.\n3. Classify risk with the kernel command. Prefer higher risk when uncertain. Example: `harness risk classify --files \"src/foo.ts\" --linesChanged 120 --confidence medium --json`.\n4. Enter the route through the kernel, not by editing files: `harness enter --root . --phase build --subPhase Execute --mode auto --riskClass M --objective \"short objective\" --json`.\n5. If `harness` is not on PATH inside this repository, use `node packages/cli/dist/index.js` with the same arguments.\n6. Verify before writing: `harness status --root . --json` and `harness runtime assess-route --root . --json`.\n7. Start implementation only after the route, risk class, operating mode, and runtime bindings are visible. Keep writes inside allowed zones from hook context.\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: development session entry, operating mode selection, risk-to-route binding, initial verification commands\n- Out of scope: manual .planning edits, OMX workflow dependency, implementation before route activation, silent risk downgrades\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: state-machine, risk-classification, runtime-bindings, gate-policy\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },

        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "bind-runtime",
        "title": "Bind Runtime",
        "relativePath": "skills/bind-runtime/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"bind-runtime\"\ndescription: \"Map runtime hook capabilities to canonical gate bindings.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=bind-runtime source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Bind Runtime\n\nMap runtime hook capabilities to canonical gate bindings.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: runtime, binding, adapter, hook\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: runtime capability inspection, gate binding status, blocking capability report\n- Out of scope: adapter file generation, platform process management, policy override\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, known_gap\n- Hooks: runtime-bindings, platform-adapters\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "build-inner-loop",
        "title": "Build Inner Loop",
        "relativePath": "skills/build-inner-loop/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"build-inner-loop\"\ndescription: \"Run a risk-calibrated RED/GREEN/REFACTOR implementation loop.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=build-inner-loop source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Build Inner Loop\n\nRun a risk-calibrated RED/GREEN/REFACTOR implementation loop.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: build\n- Gate types: pre_tool, post_tool\n- Risk classes: L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: build, tdd, implement, code\n- Automatic: false\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: test-first workflow, minimum implementation, focused verification\n- Out of scope: release decision, production monitoring, human approval\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: integration_tests, command_output, files_modified\n- Hooks: convergence, evidence-management\n- Subagents: test-writer, reviewer\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "classify-risk",
        "title": "Classify Risk",
        "relativePath": "skills/classify-risk/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"classify-risk\"\ndescription: \"Assign a canonical T/L/M/H/C risk class and operating mode to each intent.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=classify-risk source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Classify Risk\n\nAssign a canonical T/L/M/H/C risk class and operating mode to each intent.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: classify, risk, intent\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: risk classification, forcing signal scan, operating mode selection\n- Out of scope: cycle transition, implementation, evidence sufficiency evaluation\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: risk-classification\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "close-run",
        "title": "Close Run",
        "relativePath": "skills/close-run/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"close-run\"\ndescription: \"Finalize a run only after evidence, policy, and runtime gates agree.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=close-run source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Close Run\n\nFinalize a run only after evidence, policy, and runtime gates agree.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: stop\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: close, final, finish\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: final state selection, residual risk summary, known gap disclosure\n- Out of scope: additional implementation, silent evidence fabrication, risk downgrade\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: confidence_level, known_gap, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: close-finalization, evidence-management, gate-policy\n- Subagents: evidence-collector\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "gate-policy",
        "title": "Gate Policy",
        "relativePath": "skills/gate-policy/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"gate-policy\"\ndescription: \"Evaluate lifecycle gates against risk policy, runtime bindings, and allowed scope.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=gate-policy source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Gate Policy\n\nEvaluate lifecycle gates against risk policy, runtime bindings, and allowed scope.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt, pre_tool, post_tool, pre_compact, post_compact, stop, subagent_start, subagent_stop\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: gate, policy, guard\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: gate decisions, policy violations, context injection\n- Out of scope: risk scoring, subagent implementation, adapter-specific hook install\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, known_gap\n- Hooks: gate-policy, runtime-bindings, platform-adapters\n- Subagents: evidence-collector\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "hima-enter",
        "title": "HIMA Enter",
        "relativePath": "skills/hima-enter/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"hima-enter\"\ndescription: \"Start a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=hima-enter source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# HIMA Enter\n\nStart a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: hima, enter, start development, development mode, mode developpement, governed development\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Procedure\n\nUse this skill as the first explicit step before governed implementation work. Do not depend on OMX, external workflow skills, or manual edits to `.planning/`.\n\n1. Decide whether the user is asking for development. If the request is only chat, research, explanation, or status, do not enter `build/Execute`; report status instead.\n2. Choose one operating mode: `bypass` for T/L low-friction work only, `auto` as the default autonomous development mode, or `pairing` when the user wants checkpoints or the risk is H/C.\n3. Classify risk with the kernel command. Prefer higher risk when uncertain. Example: `harness risk classify --files \"src/foo.ts\" --linesChanged 120 --confidence medium --json`.\n4. Enter the route through the kernel, not by editing files: `harness enter --root . --phase build --subPhase Execute --mode auto --riskClass M --objective \"short objective\" --json`.\n5. If `harness` is not on PATH inside this repository, use `node packages/cli/dist/index.js` with the same arguments.\n6. Verify before writing: `harness status --root . --json` and `harness runtime assess-route --root . --json`.\n7. Start implementation only after the route, risk class, operating mode, and runtime bindings are visible. Keep writes inside allowed zones from hook context.\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: development session entry, operating mode selection, risk-to-route binding, initial verification commands\n- Out of scope: manual .planning edits, OMX workflow dependency, implementation before route activation, silent risk downgrades\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: state-machine, risk-classification, runtime-bindings, gate-policy\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "propose-change",
        "title": "Propose Change",
        "relativePath": "skills/propose-change/SKILL.md",

        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "hima-enter",
        "title": "HIMA Enter",
        "relativePath": "skills/hima-enter/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"hima-enter\"\ndescription: \"Start a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=hima-enter source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# HIMA Enter\n\nStart a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: hima, enter, start development, development mode, mode developpement, governed development\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Procedure\n\nUse this skill as the first explicit step before governed implementation work. Do not depend on OMX, external workflow skills, or manual edits to `.planning/`.\n\n1. Decide whether the user is asking for development. If the request is only chat, research, explanation, or status, do not enter `build/Execute`; report status instead.\n2. Choose one operating mode: `bypass` for T/L low-friction work only, `auto` as the default autonomous development mode, or `pairing` when the user wants checkpoints or the risk is H/C.\n3. Classify risk with the kernel command. Prefer higher risk when uncertain. Example: `harness risk classify --files \"src/foo.ts\" --linesChanged 120 --confidence medium --json`.\n4. Enter the route through the kernel, not by editing files: `harness enter --root . --phase build --subPhase Execute --mode auto --riskClass M --objective \"short objective\" --json`.\n5. If `harness` is not on PATH inside this repository, use `node packages/cli/dist/index.js` with the same arguments.\n6. Verify before writing: `harness status --root . --json` and `harness runtime assess-route --root . --json`.\n7. Start implementation only after the route, risk class, operating mode, and runtime bindings are visible. Keep writes inside allowed zones from hook context.\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: development session entry, operating mode selection, risk-to-route binding, initial verification commands\n- Out of scope: manual .planning edits, OMX workflow dependency, implementation before route activation, silent risk downgrades\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: state-machine, risk-classification, runtime-bindings, gate-policy\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },

        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "hima-enter",
        "title": "HIMA Enter",
        "relativePath": "skills/hima-enter/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"hima-enter\"\ndescription: \"Start a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=hima-enter source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# HIMA Enter\n\nStart a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: hima, enter, start development, development mode, mode developpement, governed development\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Procedure\n\nUse this skill as the first explicit step before governed implementation work. Do not depend on OMX, external workflow skills, or manual edits to `.planning/`.\n\n1. Decide whether the user is asking for development. If the request is only chat, research, explanation, or status, do not enter `build/Execute`; report status instead.\n2. Choose one operating mode: `bypass` for T/L low-friction work only, `auto` as the default autonomous development mode, or `pairing` when the user wants checkpoints or the risk is H/C.\n3. Classify risk with the kernel command. Prefer higher risk when uncertain. Example: `harness risk classify --files \"src/foo.ts\" --linesChanged 120 --confidence medium --json`.\n4. Enter the route through the kernel, not by editing files: `harness enter --root . --phase build --subPhase Execute --mode auto --riskClass M --objective \"short objective\" --json`.\n5. If `harness` is not on PATH inside this repository, use `node packages/cli/dist/index.js` with the same arguments.\n6. Verify before writing: `harness status --root . --json` and `harness runtime assess-route --root . --json`.\n7. Start implementation only after the route, risk class, operating mode, and runtime bindings are visible. Keep writes inside allowed zones from hook context.\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: development session entry, operating mode selection, risk-to-route binding, initial verification commands\n- Out of scope: manual .planning edits, OMX workflow dependency, implementation before route activation, silent risk downgrades\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: state-machine, risk-classification, runtime-bindings, gate-policy\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },

        "content": "<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=subagent id=evidence-collector source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Evidence Collector\n\nCollect accepted evidence and recommend final state or blocking gaps.\n\n## Spawn Rules\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: stop, subagent_stop\n- Minimum risk class: T\n- Applicability by risk: C=mandatory, H=mandatory, L=mandatory, M=mandatory, T=mandatory\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Max parallel safe: 1\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: subagent_output, known_gap, confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: state-machine, gate-policy, platform-adapters, evidence-management, close-finalization\n- Skills: transition-phase, gate-policy, validate-evidence, close-run\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "subagent",
        "id": "perf-profiler",
        "title": "Performance Profiler",
        "relativePath": "agents/perf-profiler.md",
        "content": "<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=subagent id=perf-profiler source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Performance Profiler\n\nMeasure latency, throughput, and error rates against SLOs.\n\n## Spawn Rules\n\n- Macro cycles: validation, run\n- Gate types: subagent_start, subagent_stop\n- Minimum risk class: M\n- Applicability by risk: C=mandatory, H=mandatory, L=skipped, M=optional, T=skipped\n- Operating modes: auto, pairing\n- Max parallel safe: 3\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: load_tests, command_output, subagent_output\n- Hooks: convergence, evidence-management\n- Skills: run-monitor\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "subagent",
        "id": "retro-facilitator",
        "title": "Retro Facilitator",
        "relativePath": "agents/retro-facilitator.md",
        "content": "<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=subagent id=retro-facilitator source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Retro Facilitator\n\nSummarize learning-cycle outcomes and produce action items.\n\n## Spawn Rules\n\n- Macro cycles: learning\n- Gate types: subagent_start, subagent_stop\n- Minimum risk class: M\n- Applicability by risk: C=mandatory, H=mandatory, L=skipped, M=mandatory, T=skipped\n- Operating modes: auto, pairing\n- Max parallel safe: 1\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: product_validation, subagent_output, known_gap\n- Hooks: convergence, close-finalization, evidence-management\n- Skills: validate-evidence\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "subagent",
        "id": "reviewer",
        "title": "Reviewer",
        "relativePath": "agents/reviewer.md",
        "content": "<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=subagent id=reviewer source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Reviewer\n\nPerform antagonistic code review and return an approved or changes-required verdict.\n\n## Spawn Rules\n\n- Macro cycles: build, validation\n- Gate types: subagent_start, subagent_stop\n- Minimum risk class: M\n- Applicability by risk: C=mandatory, H=mandatory, L=optional, M=mandatory, T=skipped\n- Operating modes: auto, pairing\n- Max parallel safe: 3\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: review_1, review_2_or_antagonist, subagent_output\n- Hooks: gate-policy, convergence, evidence-management\n- Skills: build-inner-loop, validate-evidence\n"
      },

        "relativePath": "agents/security-auditor.md",
        "content": "<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=subagent id=security-auditor source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Security Auditor\n\nRun extended security review for H-risk increments.\n\n## Spawn Rules\n\n- Macro cycles: validation\n- Gate types: subagent_start, subagent_stop\n- Minimum risk class: H\n- Applicability by risk: C=mandatory, H=mandatory, L=skipped, M=optional, T=skipped\n- Operating modes: auto, pairing\n- Max parallel safe: 3\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: dast_report, independent_security_audit, subagent_output\n- Hooks: evidence-management\n- Skills: validate-evidence\n"

        "content": "<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=subagent id=test-writer source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Test Writer\n\nWrite RED-phase tests from acceptance criteria before production implementation.\n\n## Spawn Rules\n\n- Macro cycles: build\n- Gate types: subagent_start, subagent_stop\n- Minimum risk class: L\n- Applicability by risk: C=mandatory, H=mandatory, L=optional, M=mandatory, T=skipped\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Max parallel safe: 1\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: integration_tests, subagent_output, command_output\n- Hooks: platform-adapters, convergence, evidence-management\n- Skills: build-inner-loop\n"

      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "bind-runtime",
        "title": "Bind Runtime",
        "relativePath": "skills/bind-runtime/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"bind-runtime\"\ndescription: \"Map runtime hook capabilities to canonical gate bindings.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=bind-runtime source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Bind Runtime\n\nMap runtime hook capabilities to canonical gate bindings.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: runtime, binding, adapter, hook\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: runtime capability inspection, gate binding status, blocking capability report\n- Out of scope: adapter file generation, platform process management, policy override\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, known_gap\n- Hooks: runtime-bindings, platform-adapters\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "build-inner-loop",
        "title": "Build Inner Loop",
        "relativePath": "skills/build-inner-loop/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"build-inner-loop\"\ndescription: \"Run a risk-calibrated RED/GREEN/REFACTOR implementation loop.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=build-inner-loop source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Build Inner Loop\n\nRun a risk-calibrated RED/GREEN/REFACTOR implementation loop.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: build\n- Gate types: pre_tool, post_tool\n- Risk classes: L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: build, tdd, implement, code\n- Automatic: false\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: test-first workflow, minimum implementation, focused verification\n- Out of scope: release decision, production monitoring, human approval\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: integration_tests, command_output, files_modified\n- Hooks: convergence, evidence-management\n- Subagents: test-writer, reviewer\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "classify-risk",
        "title": "Classify Risk",
        "relativePath": "skills/classify-risk/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"classify-risk\"\ndescription: \"Assign a canonical T/L/M/H/C risk class and operating mode to each intent.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=classify-risk source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Classify Risk\n\nAssign a canonical T/L/M/H/C risk class and operating mode to each intent.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: classify, risk, intent\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: risk classification, forcing signal scan, operating mode selection\n- Out of scope: cycle transition, implementation, evidence sufficiency evaluation\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: risk-classification\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "close-run",
        "title": "Close Run",
        "relativePath": "skills/close-run/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"close-run\"\ndescription: \"Finalize a run only after evidence, policy, and runtime gates agree.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=close-run source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Close Run\n\nFinalize a run only after evidence, policy, and runtime gates agree.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: stop\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: close, final, finish\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: final state selection, residual risk summary, known gap disclosure\n- Out of scope: additional implementation, silent evidence fabrication, risk downgrade\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: confidence_level, known_gap, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: close-finalization, evidence-management, gate-policy\n- Subagents: evidence-collector\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "gate-policy",
        "title": "Gate Policy",
        "relativePath": "skills/gate-policy/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"gate-policy\"\ndescription: \"Evaluate lifecycle gates against risk policy, runtime bindings, and allowed scope.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=gate-policy source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Gate Policy\n\nEvaluate lifecycle gates against risk policy, runtime bindings, and allowed scope.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt, pre_tool, post_tool, pre_compact, post_compact, stop, subagent_start, subagent_stop\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: gate, policy, guard\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: gate decisions, policy violations, context injection\n- Out of scope: risk scoring, subagent implementation, adapter-specific hook install\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, known_gap\n- Hooks: gate-policy, runtime-bindings, platform-adapters\n- Subagents: evidence-collector\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "hima-enter",
        "title": "HIMA Enter",
        "relativePath": "skills/hima-enter/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"hima-enter\"\ndescription: \"Start a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=hima-enter source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# HIMA Enter\n\nStart a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: hima, enter, start development, development mode, mode developpement, governed development\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Procedure\n\nUse this skill as the first explicit step before governed implementation work. Do not depend on OMX, external workflow skills, or manual edits to `.planning/`.\n\n1. Decide whether the user is asking for development. If the request is only chat, research, explanation, or status, do not enter `build/Execute`; report status instead.\n2. Choose one operating mode: `bypass` for T/L low-friction work only, `auto` as the default autonomous development mode, or `pairing` when the user wants checkpoints or the risk is H/C.\n3. Classify risk with the kernel command. Prefer higher risk when uncertain. Example: `harness risk classify --files \"src/foo.ts\" --linesChanged 120 --confidence medium --json`.\n4. Enter the route through the kernel, not by editing files: `harness enter --root . --phase build --subPhase Execute --mode auto --riskClass M --objective \"short objective\" --json`.\n5. If `harness` is not on PATH inside this repository, use `node packages/cli/dist/index.js` with the same arguments.\n6. Verify before writing: `harness status --root . --json` and `harness runtime assess-route --root . --json`.\n7. Start implementation only after the route, risk class, operating mode, and runtime bindings are visible. Keep writes inside allowed zones from hook context.\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: development session entry, operating mode selection, risk-to-route binding, initial verification commands\n- Out of scope: manual .planning edits, OMX workflow dependency, implementation before route activation, silent risk downgrades\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: state-machine, risk-classification, runtime-bindings, gate-policy\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },

        "id": "bind-runtime",
        "title": "Bind Runtime",
        "relativePath": "skills/bind-runtime/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"bind-runtime\"\ndescription: \"Map runtime hook capabilities to canonical gate bindings.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=bind-runtime source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Bind Runtime\n\nMap runtime hook capabilities to canonical gate bindings.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: runtime, binding, adapter, hook\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: runtime capability inspection, gate binding status, blocking capability report\n- Out of scope: adapter file generation, platform process management, policy override\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, known_gap\n- Hooks: runtime-bindings, platform-adapters\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",

        "relativePath": "skills/build-inner-loop/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"build-inner-loop\"\ndescription: \"Run a risk-calibrated RED/GREEN/REFACTOR implementation loop.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=build-inner-loop source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Build Inner Loop\n\nRun a risk-calibrated RED/GREEN/REFACTOR implementation loop.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: build\n- Gate types: pre_tool, post_tool\n- Risk classes: L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: build, tdd, implement, code\n- Automatic: false\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: test-first workflow, minimum implementation, focused verification\n- Out of scope: release decision, production monitoring, human approval\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: integration_tests, command_output, files_modified\n- Hooks: convergence, evidence-management\n- Subagents: test-writer, reviewer\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "classify-risk",
        "title": "Classify Risk",
        "relativePath": "skills/classify-risk/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"classify-risk\"\ndescription: \"Assign a canonical T/L/M/H/C risk class and operating mode to each intent.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=classify-risk source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Classify Risk\n\nAssign a canonical T/L/M/H/C risk class and operating mode to each intent.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: classify, risk, intent\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: risk classification, forcing signal scan, operating mode selection\n- Out of scope: cycle transition, implementation, evidence sufficiency evaluation\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: risk-classification\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "close-run",
        "title": "Close Run",
        "relativePath": "skills/close-run/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"close-run\"\ndescription: \"Finalize a run only after evidence, policy, and runtime gates agree.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=close-run source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Close Run\n\nFinalize a run only after evidence, policy, and runtime gates agree.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: stop\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: close, final, finish\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: final state selection, residual risk summary, known gap disclosure\n- Out of scope: additional implementation, silent evidence fabrication, risk downgrade\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: confidence_level, known_gap, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: close-finalization, evidence-management, gate-policy\n- Subagents: evidence-collector\n"
      },

        "title": "Gate Policy",
        "relativePath": "skills/gate-policy/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"gate-policy\"\ndescription: \"Evaluate lifecycle gates against risk policy, runtime bindings, and allowed scope.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=gate-policy source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Gate Policy\n\nEvaluate lifecycle gates against risk policy, runtime bindings, and allowed scope.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt, pre_tool, post_tool, pre_compact, post_compact, stop, subagent_start, subagent_stop\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: gate, policy, guard\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: gate decisions, policy violations, context injection\n- Out of scope: risk scoring, subagent implementation, adapter-specific hook install\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, known_gap\n- Hooks: gate-policy, runtime-bindings, platform-adapters\n- Subagents: evidence-collector\n"
      },
      {
        "kind": "skill",
        "id": "hima-enter",
        "title": "HIMA Enter",
        "relativePath": "skills/hima-enter/SKILL.md",
        "content": "---\nname: \"hima-enter\"\ndescription: \"Start a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=hima-enter source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# HIMA Enter\n\nStart a governed HIMA development session from an idea, select the operating mode, and bind the route before implementation.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: session_start, user_prompt\n- Risk classes: T, L, M, H, C\n- Operating modes: bypass, auto, pairing\n- Keywords: hima, enter, start development, development mode, mode developpement, governed development\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Procedure\n\nUse this skill as the first explicit step before governed implementation work. Do not depend on OMX, external workflow skills, or manual edits to `.planning/`.\n\n1. Decide whether the user is asking for development. If the request is only chat, research, explanation, or status, do not enter `build/Execute`; report status instead.\n2. Choose one operating mode: `bypass` for T/L low-friction work only, `auto` as the default autonomous development mode, or `pairing` when the user wants checkpoints or the risk is H/C.\n3. Classify risk with the kernel command. Prefer higher risk when uncertain. Example: `harness risk classify --files \"src/foo.ts\" --linesChanged 120 --confidence medium --json`.\n4. Enter the route through the kernel, not by editing files: `harness enter --root . --phase build --subPhase Execute --mode auto --riskClass M --objective \"short objective\" --json`.\n5. If `harness` is not on PATH inside this repository, use `node packages/cli/dist/index.js` with the same arguments.\n6. Verify before writing: `harness status --root . --json` and `harness runtime assess-route --root . --json`.\n7. Start implementation only after the route, risk class, operating mode, and runtime bindings are visible. Keep writes inside allowed zones from hook context.\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: development session entry, operating mode selection, risk-to-route binding, initial verification commands\n- Out of scope: manual .planning edits, OMX workflow dependency, implementation before route activation, silent risk downgrades\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, confidence_level, risk_remaining\n- Hooks: state-machine, risk-classification, runtime-bindings, gate-policy\n- Subagents: none\n"
      },
      {

        "content": "---\nname: \"transition-phase\"\ndescription: \"Guard cycle movement with state-machine and evidence checks.\"\n---\n\n<!-- HIMA:CATALOG-ARTIFACT kind=skill id=transition-phase source=operational-catalog -->\n\n# Transition Phase\n\nGuard cycle movement with state-machine and evidence checks.\n\n## Activation\n\n- Macro cycles: discovery, cadrage, conception, build, validation, release, run, learning\n- Gate types: post_tool, stop\n- Risk classes: M, H, C\n- Operating modes: auto, pairing\n- Keywords: advance, next cycle, promote, transition\n- Automatic: true\n\n## Ownership\n\n- Owns: state transition, exit condition check, missing evidence report\n- Out of scope: artifact creation, risk demotion, runtime installation\n\n## References\n\n- Evidence produced: hook_decision, known_gap\n- Hooks: state-machine, convergence, close-finalization\n- Subagents: evidence-collector\n"
